The single-edge test answers the "which miRNA / just an edge?" question for the translational layer; the miR-17~92→PTEN protein-residual hit is the strongest external corroboration of the basal-hub thesis. **Caveats:** per-PAM50 n is small (Basal ≈16) so subtype p-values are individually NS — `top_pam50` is the descriptive strongest-coupling lineage, the FDR claim is the cohort-level driver. "Literature-validated" = miRTarBase functional-MTI / breast PMID (the repo's curated layer), not a fresh per-edge search. **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`):** all **17/17 FDR driver arms hold** under the literal 15-plex TMT batch adjustment (none drop); `cptac_validation/prospective/resid_survivors/batch_plex/`. |
| MH-36 | **Orphan (non-miRTarBase) edges DO surface novel protein-level candidate edges in CPTAC — a 93-edge translational candidate queue, with the method self-validated by recovering miR-29→collagen de novo.** Screening **20,663 orphan edges** (TargetScan `ts_weight≥0.25` OR ENCORI `clipExpNum≥2`, absent from miRTarBase functional-MTI) over the Hallmark universe against CPTAC protein (cov-residualized partial Spearman; prospective primary + TCGA-105 replication direction): **15,512 testable → 539 protein-anti-correlation candidates, 182 translational (`protein_resid`) candidates, and 93 translational AND TCGA-replicated** (the strongest tier). **76/93 are fully uncurated** (no miRTarBase row at all) and **29/93 carry both priors** (sequence + CLIP). **Method validation:** the **miR-29 family→ECM/collagen axis emerges de novo** — miR-29a-3p→COL11A1 (ts_weight 0.80, clipExpNum 15), →COL6A2, →BMP1; miR-29b/c→COL11A1 — a textbook miRNA-ECM axis uncurated at these specific targets. Other dual-prior hits: miR-17-5p→DPYSL2/NEDD4L (hub family, new targets), miR-15/16→FKBP1A/ARHGDIA/AP2B1, miR-30e→MINPP1/VIM, miR-150-5p→SPARC, miR-301a-3p→ARHGEF12. | P/H (nominations) | `cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/{orphan_candidates.tsv,orphan_edges_tested.tsv.gz,method_manifest.json}` | Module `cptac_orphan_discovery.py`. Protein-layer analogue of MH-26's `mirtar_gap_coupling_queue`, but the **`protein_resid` (translational, beyond-mRNA) + two-cohort** filters make these stronger than the prior mRNA-only orphan hits. **These are wet-lab NOMINATIONS (CLIP/luciferase), NOT validated edges:** protein anti-correlation ≠ direct edge — co-expression confound persists (esp. ECM/collagen genes co-vary with stroma despite purity+CIN adjustment), and seed-family arms (miR-29a/b/c) cannot be separated. The de-novo recovery of miR-29→collagen is the credibility anchor; the 76 fully-uncurated dual-prior translational hits are the discovery surface. **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`):** under the literal 15-plex TMT batch the candidate *counts* grow (all-candidate transl+TCGA-replicated 93→122; the **fully-uncurated** "no-miRTarBase-studies" subset 76→105) but **membership churns ~36%**: of the 76 fully-uncurated strongest-tier edges only **49 persist**, 27 drop, 56 new. The **miR-29→collagen anchor persists/expands**; several drops are the **MH-39 composition artifacts** (miR-142-3p→NCK2/STAM2/TPM4, miR-103a→VCL) — plex partly captures composition/accrual. The **prediction-only tier** (TS-only, no CLIP, no studies — the MH-39 set) is cleaner: translational+replicated 21→29 with **16/21 persisting**, and the 5 drops are *exactly* the MH-39 composition artifacts (miR-103a→VCL, miR-142-3p→NCK2/STAM2/TPM4) while the PMM1 (miR-15a/16/195)/PPM1A (miR-19a/b) core holds — MS-batch independently reproduces the composition story. The TS-only oncomiR-family **protein**-tier edges **miR-20a/93→MMP2** also survive (ρ −0.46/−0.40 → −0.38/−0.41, q sig, replicated). **Caveat:** the count growth is partly anticonservative — `_spearman_p` uses n−2 dof and does *not* deduct the 15 plex dummies, so trust the persistors + named anchors over the raw count. `cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/batch_plex/`. |

| MH-37 | **The miRNA→target anti-correlation replicates in an independent breast cohort of new patients (Buffa 2011).** Buffa (GEO GSE22216 miRNA + GSE22219 mRNA, **207 paired primary breast tumours, no TCGA overlap**) reproduces the TCGA edge coupling: across 3,613 shared HE edges the **Buffa-vs-TCGA partial-ρ concordance is +0.32** (Spearman) and **67% of TCGA neg-sig repressive edges keep a negative sign in Buffa** (both adjusted for an E2F/G2M proliferation proxy + ER status; Buffa lacks purity/HRD). Concordance is stable when ER is added (+0.335→+0.319), i.e. not a subtype-composition artifact. Independently, **HE edges couple more than TargetScan-orphan edges** (Buffa neg-sig frac 0.052 vs 0.032), reproducing the MH-23 HE>orphan gap in a second cohort. | S/R | `output/buffa_validation/{he_edge_coupling_buffa_vs_tcga,orphan_edge_coupling_buffa}.tsv`, `figures/buffa_vs_tcga_replication.png` | **Independent-patient leg** the same-patient CPTAC protein layer (MH-34) cannot give. Caveats: n=207, Illumina **microarray** miRNA (legacy bare arm names → mapped by bare stem, guide-arm-accurate, passenger-arm edges approximate), proliferation+ER adjustment only; **only 13% of TCGA neg-sig edges reach FDR-significance in Buffa** (smaller n, different platform) — so the replication headline is **sign-concordance / ρ-concordance, not significance count**. **Critical method note:** the GEO title index (`BCmicroRNA N` vs `BCmRNA N`) is NOT the patient pairing (scrambles to ρ≈0, sign-concord 0.50); samples matched by clinical fingerprint (age/size/nodes/ER/grade/DRFS), 207/210 unique. |

| MH-38 | **⛔ RETRACTED (MH-114, 2026-07-12; retraction applied here 2026-07-17 — it had been recorded ONLY inside MH-114's row for 5 days while this row still read S/R and the talk still cited it).** **The screen that produced these 594 candidates had NO cell-composition block** — `analyses/cptac/cptac_orphan_discovery.py` built `purity+cin` straight off the clinical frame and never called `_covariates`. Re-run composition-adjusted: **594 → 23 candidates** (kept 20, lost 574, 3 new). **The fixed 23-row list contains ZERO collagen and ZERO ECM edges — the entire miR-29→ECM axis that WAS this claim's headline (34 of the 108) is gone from the nomination list.** This is what MH-112 predicts (90.5% of `composition_explained` edges are OPPOSITE-compartment: miRNA epithelial, collagen stromal ⇒ bulk-mixing arithmetic) and what MH-114's shuffled null proved (site-free non-edges reproduce the real compartment gradient exactly, −0.1290 vs −0.1272). ⚠ **`output/buffa_validation/*` is still dated 2026-06-26 and has NOT been re-run**, so the "108" below is computed from the retracted 594-row input. **Do not cite 108.** Corroborated independently and 16 days EARLY by **MH-55** (`dcis_orphan_rigor`, 2026-06-26): ECM/collagen orphans preferentially attenuate under composition adjustment (neg 84%→62%, median ρ halved) — and note the fixed 23-list does **not** contain MH-55's "surviving core" (let-7d→COL1A2, miR-29a→COL1A1, miR-128→FBLN2) either. **✅ RE-RUN 2026-07-17 — MEASURED, NOT EXPECTED: 108 → 3.** `triple_validated` **108 → 3**; **ECM/collagen among triple-validated 30 → 0** (all rows: 64 → 0). The 3 survivors are all miRGeneDB **guide** arms: **miR-195-5p→PSMD7** (buffa ρ=−0.20, cptac −0.40, tcga −0.06), **miR-181b-5p→IRS2** (−0.02/−0.45/−0.13), **miR-22-3p→STK39** (−0.01/−0.39/−0.32); only **1** is neg-sig in Buffa. Funnel: 23 candidates → 20 genuine → 11 testable in Buffa → 3 also CPTAC-sig + TCGA-replicated → 3 same-sign (100%). **The pre-registered expectation ("single digits, possibly zero ECM") was CORRECT.** ⚠ **THE RE-RUN REQUIRED A CODE FIX — the module had been DEAD, not merely stale:** `GEO_DIR` was built as `Path(__file__).parent.parent/"data"`, correct when the module lived at the subproject top level, but it was later moved into `eval/` — which added a directory level and pointed it at the non-existent `mirna_hallmark/data/`. Every input raised `FileNotFoundError`; the data was at the repo root all along. Fixed to `C.REPO_ROOT / "data" / ...` (config is the single source of truth for paths; every other path in the module already used `C.*`). **CONTROL — the HE-edge legs are untouched and reproduce exactly** (concordance **+0.319**, sign-concordance **0.593**, TCGA neg-sig same-sign **0.673**, neg-sig-in-Buffa **0.128**, HE 0.052 vs orphan 0.032), confirming the fix perturbed only the orphan lane. Superseded claim retained verbatim below for provenance. ⟶ ORIGINAL CLAIM (RETRACTED): **Genuine orphan edges — TargetScan/ENCORI-predicted, completely absent from miRTarBase — validate across THREE independent readouts (TCGA mRNA · CPTAC protein · Buffa mRNA).** Of 594 CPTAC orphan candidates, **492 are genuine orphans** (`mirtar_any==False`, no miRTarBase study at all). Of those that were CPTAC-protein-significant AND TCGA-replicated and testable in the independent Buffa cohort (n=127), **108 (85%) keep a negative sign in Buffa** — far above the 50% chance rate — giving **108 triple-cohort-validated orphan edges** (47 reach FDR-significance in Buffa). **All 108 are miRGeneDB high-confidence guide arms** (37 miRNAs, 67 genes), i.e. legitimate miRNAs (in the curated ~65%) acting on targets uncurated by miRTarBase. The set is led by the **miR-29 family → ECM/collagen** axis (29 edges: COL1A2/COL5A1/COL5A3/COL6A2/COL6A3/COL11A1, FSTL1, BMP1, LOXL2, CALU) — 34/108 are ECM targets — plus miR-205→TXNRD1, miR-150→SPARC, miR-15/16→AHNAK/FERMT2. | **⛔ RETRACTED** (was S/R — the tag a reader trusts; downgraded 2026-07-17) | `output/buffa_validation/orphan_triple_cohort_validation.tsv` | Extends MH-26/MH-37 and the CPTAC `cptac_orphan_discovery`. These are **curation-gap edges with the strongest possible cross-cohort support short of luciferase** — real miRNAs, real targets, coherent repression in 3 independent datasets/2 layers, but still **nominations** (coupling ≠ direct binding; miR-29→collagen is established R, the rest a prioritised CLIP/luciferase queue). 85% Buffa same-sign >> the 67% HE-edge rate (MH-37) because these were pre-selected for 2-cohort coupling. **All-guide is partly a measurement artifact, NOT proof non-miRGeneDB arms fail:** the pool had 26 non-guide genuine orphans (16 true double-orphans not in miRGeneDB, 8 passenger, 2 ambiguous); none triple-validated, but **14/16 double-orphans are not even probed by the Buffa Illumina v1 array** (rare/non-canonical arms), and the 2 testable (miR-520b→WFS1/AKT1) were not TCGA-replicated and went positive in Buffa. So the funnel is structurally biased toward guide arms (cross-cohort coupling + array coverage both favour real miRNAs). Reinforces miRGeneDB-as-filter (cf. MH-32) but a fair double-orphan test needs a cohort that *measures* them (miRNA-seq, not microarray). |

| MH-39 | **Cell-composition adjustment splits the prediction-only orphan tier into a cell-intrinsic core and a stromal/immune-lineage artifact (pilot).** Re-testing the 24 prediction-only (TargetScan-sequence-only, no CLIP, no miRTarBase) translational+TCGA-replicated orphans (MH-36) after adding **PAM50 + CAF / endothelial / immune marker composite scores** to the residualization (base purity+CIN prospective / CPE+HRD tcga105): **17/24 `protein_resid` survivors hold** in prospective (q<0.1), **18/21 stay negative** in tcga105. The **7 drops are exactly the composition-loaded targets**: VCL (CAF ρ=0.79, endo 0.54), TPM4 (CAF 0.67), HOOK3/RNH1 (CAF ~0.49), and the **miR-142-3p cluster WASL/STAM2** (load *negatively* on immune, −0.40/−0.32 — miR-142 is a hematopoietic-lineage arm, so the "edge" was immune-content co-variation). The robust core is **metabolic/composition-neutral**: the **miR-15/16/195→PMM1** convergence (q≈0.002, CAF-neutral after adjustment), **miR-19a/b-3p→PPM1A** (CAF ρ=0.06), miR-107→VCAN (survives despite stromal context), let-7g→CERCAM, miR-532→PURA, miR-101→RAB1A. | P/H (nominations) | `cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/confound_pilot/{pilot_edge_confound_comparison,pilot_marker_loadings}.tsv` | Module `cptac_orphan_confound_pilot.py` (`--use-estimate` toggles the fraction source). **Pilot scope** — 24-edge prediction-only tier only. **Robust to the deconvolution method:** repeating with **true ESTIMATE ssGSEA fractions** (ImmuneScore+StromalScore, R `estimate`, run per cohort on its own RNA; StromalScore folds CAF+endothelium, endothelial marker kept separately) gives **16/24** prospective survivors (vs 17 with marker composites) and **18/21** tcga105 — same robust core (PMM1, PPM1A, VCAN, CERCAM), same drops (VCL, TPM4, miR-142-3p cluster). Tightens MH-36: the strongest discovery tier is real but ~29% is cell-composition; **PMM1/PPM1A are the composition-robust de-novo nominations**, method-independent. Next: extend the same confounder set to the full 93/594 orphan candidates and to the curated `protein_resid` survivors (MH-35). **Widest-confounder evidence table** (`cptac_orphan_evidence_table.py`) adds **target-gene CN** (the missing covariate) to the full stack (base + PAM50 + ESTIMATE Immune/Stromal + endo + target CN), across TCGA + both CPTAC RNA and CPTAC protein/`resid`: the core (miR-195/16/15a→PMM1, PPM1A, NCK2, KIF1B, VCAN) **survives even with target CN partialled**; the PMM1 edges are mRNA-flat / protein-strong = translational. **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`, plex added to base cov):** with composition on top of base+TMT-plex, prospective resid survivors **24→13** and tcga105 **21→20**; the same composition artifacts drop (VCL/WASL/STAM2/TPM4/HOOK3) and the PMM1/PPM1A core holds — the composition story is unchanged by MS-batch adjustment; `confound_pilot/batch_plex/`. |

| MH-40 | **The translational survivor couplings are TARGET-SPECIFIC, not a global anti-correlation artifact — each driver arm anti-correlates with its cognate protein but NOT with the unrelated proteome.** Decoy/placebo control on the MH-35 survivor drivers (prospective): for each driver arm, the cognate `protein_resid` coupling is compared against the *same* arm's coupling to every Hallmark-universe protein it does **not** target (miRTarBase any-evidence ∪ TargetScan seed ∪ ENCORI CLIP all excluded; ≥50 decoys; purity+CIN-residualized partial Spearman, same machinery as MH-35/36). **19 edges testable (13 FDR drivers). The cognate couplings (median ρ=−0.33) sit ~16× more negative than the unrelated decoy null (grand decoy median ρ=−0.021), which is centred essentially at zero — unrelated proteins are NOT broadly negative.** Per-arm, **9/13 FDR drivers are specific** (cognate in the decoy left tail, empirical p<0.05: KRT8←miR-186-5p, DDAH1←miR-210-3p, BMP1←miR-194-5p, PEBP1←miR-155-5p, ARHGDIA←miR-151a-5p, HMGN2←miR-23a-3p, MINPP1←miR-30b-5p, SLPI←miR-146a-5p, MTCH2←miR-135b-5p) and **12/13 at p<0.10**. The 2 borderline cases are exactly the broad proliferation oncomiRs whose decoy nulls are the most-negative of any arm — **PTEN←miR-17-5p (emp p=0.062; decoy median −0.060)** and ZYX←miR-16-5p (0.060) — i.e. the control correctly *discounts* arms that are genuinely somewhat globally anti-correlated, exactly as a specificity test should. **Batch-confounder control (literal TMT plex):** adding the **literal prospective TMT-plex dummies** (15 plexes; PDC study PDC000120, fetched by `scripts/cptac/fetch_cptac_brca_tmt_plex.py`; the canonical proteomics batch) to the purity+CIN residualization leaves **8/9 specific edges plex-robust** — only **MTCH2←miR-135b-5p** drops (cognate ρ −0.26→−0.21, emp p 0.034→0.094). Notably **HMGN2←miR-23a-3p survives the literal plex** (it had dropped under the coarser enrolling-**site** proxy, which over-aggregates 6 site dummies and knocked out 7/9→ HMGN2+MTCH2). So the precise batch adjustment is *cleaner* than the proxy and the survivor core (KRT8, MINPP1, DDAH1, ARHGDIA, BMP1, PEBP1, SLPI, HMGN2) is confirmed target-specific even after removing MS-plex structure. | **S** (specificity) | `cptac_validation/prospective/target_specificity/{arm_specificity.tsv,decoy_couplings.tsv.gz,method_manifest.json}` | Module `cptac_target_specificity.py` (batch pass default `--batch-kind plex`; `site`/`none`/`auto` selectable; `arm_specificity.tsv` carries `*_batch` + `batch_robust` columns). Batch helpers shared via `mirna_hallmark/cptac_batch.py` and **propagated to MH-34/35/36/39** (`--batch-kind plex` writes a `batch_plex/` subdir per module; prospective = TMT plex PDC000120, tcga105 = iTRAQ `Experiment Order` from the Sample-Mapping xlsx). Directly answers the reviewer worry that the MH-35 protein-residual couplings could be a confound (proliferation / purity / cell-composition / MS batch) that suppresses the whole proteome — they are not: the negativity is concentrated on the cognate target and survives the literal plex. Complements MH-39 (composition covariates on the *orphan* tier) by attacking the confound from the **target-selectivity** side on the *curated* survivors. **Caveats:** seed-family-shared targets only partly removed (TargetScan-seed exclusion); the decoy-vs-0 Wilcoxon is over-powered (the *magnitude* gap is the read, not its p); protein anti-correlation still ≠ direct binding (MH-35 ceiling). PTEN/miR-17-5p remains a real edge (literature + MH-35 FDR) whose specificity is merely diluted by the cluster's broad oncomiR action. |
| MH-41 | **The cohort-wide signed-NMF gain backbone is a flat, pan-subtype `miR-21/26a/let-7`-driven program led by RASGRP1/MSH2/CCL20/VHL/HPGD — *not* the LIFR/ABCE1/IGFBP5 factor the prior report named (a stale-fit artifact, now corrected).** The signed (gain+loss) gene-pressure NMF is dominated by **one** factor (S3, mean H-activation ≈2× any other) that is the *most-shared, least-divergent* factor: flat across PAM50 (.27/.27/.30/.15, so its `Her2:KRAS_SIGNALING_UP` label is a thin-margin argmax, not real Her2 specificity), dominant in **725/1096** tumours, **45% of tumours at ≥50% share**. The §2 acquired oncomiR genes (LIFR/ABCE1/IGFBP5; IL12A/PCBP1/TPM1) are *distinct minor* factors (S9 `Basal:MYC`; S2/S8 `ESTROGEN`). **Cause of the prior error:** `nmf_sample_signatures` re-fit its **own** signed NMF independent of `mirna_comovement`'s, and the two fits returned permutation-arbitrary factor indices, so a bare-index read crossed the per-sample dominance table with the loadings/factor_subtype tables and mislabelled the backbone. **Fix:** comovement now persists full `W`/`H`; sample_signatures reuses them — one fit, identical indices+labels everywhere (join by `factor_label`, never F-index). **Unchanged interpretation:** still one shared "everything-up" gain backbone (no organised relief program anywhere; gain-mass .93–.99 across all 12 factors), with the subtype signal living in the **high-KW secondaries** — Basal MYC (S12, .51 Basal, q8e-95) + Basal E2F (S1, .51, q8e-76) = a *proliferation* axis, vs Luminal/Her2 hormone+immune secondaries; universal driver core `miR-26a-5p`+`let-7i-5p` (top-8 of all 12 factors), distinctive arm in the tail. | S (structural) / methods-fix | `tissue_reference/mirna_comovement/nmf_gene_pressure_signed_{W,H,loadings,factor_subtype}.tsv`, `nmf_sample_signatures/{summary_all,per_sample/gene_pressure_signed_cohort}.tsv` | Corrects `LANDSCAPE_REPORT.md §6b` (backbone identity + full 12-factor catalog added). NMF "dominance" on the gain axis is a *universal co-directional acquisition*, **not** discrete patient subtypes — partly methodological (one-signed gain matrix). Factor F-indices remain permutation-arbitrary across rebuilds; the stable handle is `factor_label`. |
| MH-42 | **Within-pathway NMF resolves sub-modules inside a single Hallmark program — and the CANONICAL z-spine pressure (not abundance) is the resolving lens, with a competitive-restructuring view (miR-21 gains / miR-29 loses grip) from a second edge-level family.** Restricting the matrix to one set's genes (33/50 sets ≥40 genes; per-set elbow k) surfaces structure the cross-set NMF averages away, across **two families**. **Family A (gene×sample pressure, 5 flavours × cohort+4 PAM50):** with per-set auto-k the chosen k *is* the sub-structure signal — the **z-spine (`softmax_z_logrpm`, the canonical coupling pressure) and hybrid resolve most (elbow k≈5, ~2.6–2.9 effective sub-modules)** while **abundance (no-z) collapses to ~one module (k=2, eff 1.7)** because the few high-abundance arms own the set's pressure; acquired/signed are intermediate (k≈4). *This corrects the earlier fixed-k=4 prototype reading that "abundance resolves" — under a principled elbow the no-z abundance has the LEAST resolvable structure, vindicating inclusion of the z-spine/hybrid flavours.* Sub-modules are sharp and subtype-split: **EMT (spine)** → inflammatory-contractile MYLK/INHBA/VEGFA/IL6 (Basal, q1e-109) · **miR-29a/b/c→collagen** COL4A1/SPARC/FBN1/COL5A2 (pan-subtype, KW-non-sig — recovers the orphan-discovery miR-29→ECM axis MH-36/38 AND is correctly flagged stromal-composition per MH-39) · miR-30/182 mesenchymal-marker; **E2F (spine)** → Basal replication RAD21/STMN1 (miR-92a/197) vs LumA mitotic RRM2/TOP2A (let-7) vs splicing/checkpoint. **Within-subtype** re-fit adds structure for some programs (APOPTOSIS eff 1.5 cohort → 3.2 inside Her2; HYPOXIA → 3.6 inside LumB). **Family B (edge-level miRNA×gene lenses — different matrix, no sample axis): aggregate / share / grip_change.** The **share** lens shows the miR-29 family *owns* EMT's collagen budget; the **grip_change** lens (Δshare tumor−gtex) gives a clean restructuring story — in EMT **miR-21/miR-17-3p GAIN grip** (RHOB/TGFBI/COL4A1/VEGFA/TIMP3) while **miR-29a/b/c LOSE grip** on collagen (FBN1/COL4A1/COL5A2/SPARC), and in E2F miR-21/449a/b gain grip (MSH2/CDC25A/MYC/CHEK1) vs miR-7/1246 losing it — control handed from suppressor families to the oncomiR. | P (prototype) / S (sub-module + grip recovery) | `tissue_reference/within_pathway_nmf/{factor_summary,set_dominance,lens_factors,per_sample.tsv.gz}.tsv` + `figures/` | Module `within_pathway_nmf.py` (`--sets`, `--flavours`, `--kmax`, `--min-genes`, `--no-within-subtype`); wired into `run_tissue_reference_full.sh` step 12. Complements §6 (cross-set co-variation) and §7 (OmniPath topology) with **within-program** lenses neither has. miR-29→collagen recovery is internal validation. **Caveats:** prototype (not a coupling claim); k elbow-chosen not cross-validated; sub-modules share the universal driver core (miR-26a/let-7) so read the *distinctive* arms; pressure flavours partly composition-driven (collagen especially, MH-39); grip_change is a share-Δ descriptive, not an FDR test. |
| MH-43 | **End-to-end CN→regulatory-network attribution: the CN-driven component of miRNA dosage reproduces ~half-to-two-thirds of the bulk repressive network, it is a combined/arm-scale (not single-locus) effect, and it is luminal- and immune-hot-concentrated while Basal's network is comparatively expression-driven.** Splitting each regulator arm's log2RPM per-arm into a CN-driven (`cn_hat`=OLS on its own CN) vs CN-residual component and propagating each through the **same spine pressure** (`softmax_z_logrpm`+`evidence_mass`, M0, AGO-gated) against target RNA (partial CPE+HRD+target-CN): cohort (n=1,408 genes) the `total` track gives **383** neg-FDR genes (reproduces `target_combined_anticorr` 373 ✓), the **`cn` track alone gives 744**, and **median `cn_attribution`=0.70** among genuine total-neg genes (**57.7%** are CN-driven). **It SURVIVES the full cumulative control stack: 0.54 after broad aneuploidy** (Taylor `thornsson_aneuploidy_score`) **and 0.51 after also the E2F/G2M proliferation metagene** (`cn`-track 744→665→**675**). **Proliferation is a *suppressor*, not the confound** — adjusting for it *raises* CN-driven counts (cohort 665→675; **Basal 127→262, Her2 17→72**), the same MH-17 structure — so it *masks* CN's effect rather than mimicking it, settling the metabolic-shadow caveat. **Context:** CN-attribution is highest in **LumA (0.56/0.53)** and **immune C1/C2 (0.56–0.57 / 0.42–0.48)** and survives aneuploidy ~91%; **Basal is low (0.29; only 34% CN-driven)** = expression-driven/selective; **LumB** is many-but-weak (0.24). **Granularity (combined regulator CN-load vs target RNA, n=1,335):** combined multi-locus **`locus` load (900 neg-FDR, ρ̃=−0.156) beats `single_best` (842, −0.141)** — a set property, not a single hairpin; broad **`chrom` load is strongest (941, −0.164)**; all granularities **survive the full stack and sharpen** (`locus_full` 852/−0.160, `chrom_full` 886/−0.170 — stronger median ρ than baseline) → a real locus-specific multi-locus component beneath the CIN tide. | **S** (attribution + granularity) | `cn_dosage_attribution/{cn_attribution_cohort,cn_attribution_by_context,cn_attribution_summary,cn_load_granularity_cohort,cn_load_granularity_summary}.tsv`, `MIRNA_CNV_DOSAGE_REPORT.md §12` | Module `cn_dosage_attribution.py` (covariate stack: baseline `*`, `*_aneu`, `*_full`; aneuploidy + proliferation exposed via `data_loaders.load_clinical_strata`). Closes the Link-1 (CN→expr, MH-4) + Link-2 (pressure→target, MH-1/2) gap into one end-to-end measure. **Caveat:** the raw `cn`-track surplus (744 vs 383) is partly a denoised-instrument power gain + a flat-`total` metabolic tail (DLD/FH/FXN/MCL1) — quote the **attribution among total-neg genes (0.70→0.51 full)**, not the raw `cn` count. The proliferation control (a suppressor) resolves the earlier metabolic-shadow worry. Remaining: bulk-RNA ceiling (association, not binding); broad ASCAT segments limit focal-selection claims. |

| MH-47 | **The pressure construction is validated out-of-sample and robust to its hand-tuned constants — and the promiscuity denominator is re-justified on the *attribution* axis after the held-out showed it is coupling-neutral.** **(a) Held-out (5-fold patient CV, 8 constructions, n=1041):** the candidate ranking reproduces *perfectly* out-of-sample (train→test rank-ρ **1.0**, selection stability **1.0**) → the spine construction is **not overfit** to in-sample coupling (the circularity worry is refuted) — but it is **not coupling-maximal** either: bare `softmax_z_logrpm|none` is best (test mean partial-ρ −0.0265) and the spine `…|evidence_mass` is **3rd** (−0.0256), a consistent but negligible 0.0009 gap (~1.5% of strong-neg genes). **(b) Constant sensitivity** (±25% log-normal jitter of all evidence weights + systematic ±50% per weight + AGO-weight grid): the coupling headline is **robust** — `n_strong_neg` stays within **0.90–1.01×** baseline (CV 0.06), mean-ρ CV 0.01. **(c) Attribution sweep (1,408 genes):** the denominator is coupling-neutral **and specificity-invariant** (spec rank-ρ exactly **1.0** — `D(m)` cancels in the within-arm ratio) but **materially reshapes cross-arm attribution** — it re-assigns a gene's dominant regulator for **~11%** of genes (dominant-arm concordance 0.89 vs `none`) and de-concentrates the hub structure (Gini **0.570→0.538**, dominance spread from **~43→~56** arms). **Net:** the construction generalizes (non-circular); the denominator earns its place as *promiscuity control on attribution*, not as a coupling lever, and `evidence_mass` is a principled middle (a pure `degree` penalty de-concentrates marginally more), not a uniquely optimal pick. | **S** (methods/validation) | `held_out_tuning/{per_candidate,per_fold,summary.json}`, `pressure_constant_sensitivity/{sensitivity_scores.tsv,summary.json}`, `denominator_attribution_sweep/{attribution_by_denominator.tsv,summary.json}` | Modules `held_out_tuning.py`, `pressure_constant_sensitivity.py`, `denominator_attribution_sweep.py`. **Corrects** the §3.3 doc rationale (was "sharpest/most stable coupling, Basal ≥42/50" — a coupling claim the held-out refutes; ## Open hypotheses (untested here)

| id | hypothesis |
|----|------------|
| MH-H1 | Strata with high regulatory-miRNA expression for a Hallmark show lower target-gene expression *within stratum* (stratum-resolved interaction, not just cohort). |
| MH-H2 | miRNA-locus loss co-occurs with target-program up-regulation for specific high-evidence families (loss-of-repression). |
| MH-H3 | RISC capacity (AGO gate) modifies the pressure↔expression slope more strongly in immune-cold (Thornsson) strata. |
| MH-103 | **The CPTAC protein layer CONFIRMS the mRNA-mediated model and BOUNDS the translational residual below a 101-patient cohort's resolution: miRNA dose adds NOTHING to protein beyond the gene's own transcript in 16/17 genes — and the prior positive evidence for a `βᵗ` latent was a LEAK.** The proposed protein channel's centrepiece — `βᵗ`, a per-seed-family *translational* repression latent that RNA-seq is structurally blind to — is **NOT SUPPORTED at n=101, in ANY framing.** **The leak:** the earlier evidence (OOF 9/9 positive; PTEN z=3.1; PEBP1 z=3.6) built the target `protein_resid = P − a·M` with the mediator slope **`a` fitted on ALL samples**, then OOF-ed `X → protein_resid` — so the mediator saw the test folds. **Fit `a` INSIDE the fold and the signal disappears.** **Leak-free, four independent framings agree:** (a) ridge, all ~60 families, λ swept with best-λ chosen *on the test metric* (deliberately optimistic) → median incremental OOF gain **+0.001**; (b) **NNLS — the model's OWN non-negative estimator, correctly regularized for p≈63>n=101 — all families → z>2 in 0/10 genes**; (c) NNLS restricted to the **top-3 families by TCGA β** (a strong NON-circular prior: different cohort AND different layer; K chosen post-hoc ⇒ optimistic) → z>2 in 3/10 (BCL2 3.62 · CDKN1A 2.30 · PDCD4 2.23), tiny effects d=0.01–0.05; (d) **the AGGREGATE frame — the MAXIMUM-POWER test and the one MH-34 actually used: predictor = `X_cptac·β_TCGA`, weights FIXED from another cohort and layer, ZERO parameters fitted in CPTAC ⇒ 1 df instead of 60** → **BH q<0.10 in 1/17 genes: BCL2 alone** (d=+0.028, q≈0.085, itself marginal). **PTEN — cited as the headline throughout the plan — is DEAD in every framing (aggregate d=−0.006, p=0.82).** ⇒ the negative is **NOT a leak, NOT an estimator choice, and NOT a p>n artifact — it is the TRUE EFFECT SIZE meeting the cohort size.** **Fully coherent with our own prior biology: MH-34 established repression is predominantly mRNA-mediated with a translational residual in 11/1132 ≈ 1% of genes** — on a 17-gene panel that predicts ~0.2 hits and we found 1. **There was never a `βᵗ` FIELD to fit; the plan mistook a ~1%-prevalence phenomenon for a latent dimension. No method fixes this — only n does.** Association (MH-34/35, marginal, FDR) is **NOT overturned** — and this was **verified, not argued**: re-running **MH-34's OWN frame** (partial Spearman(aggregate pressure, protein | mRNA, C)) on the same 17-gene panel with the proper C gives **13/17 NEGATIVE = repression-directed (binomial p≈0.025 — a real directional signal)** but **p<0.05 in only 1/17 (KRT8) and BH q<0.10 in 0/17** ⇒ the partial-correlation frame **AGREES** with the OOF frame. MH-34 ran on **1,132 genes → 11–29 hits ≈ 1–2%**; on 17 genes that predicts **0.2–0.4** hits and we see 0–1 — **quantitatively consistent**. ⚠ An earlier wording ("miRNA adds NOTHING to protein beyond the transcript") **over-claimed and is corrected**: the translational component is **real, weak and widespread-in-DIRECTION**, but **not resolvable per gene at n=101**. **The falsification is of the MODELING OBJECT (a βᵗ latent), not of the biology and not of MH-34.** | **S** (four leak-free out-of-fold tests with permutation nulls + BH-FDR, on real CPTAC miRNA+mRNA+protein, n=101; the max-power aggregate framing is decisive and pre-specified) | `learned/cptac_data.py` (Model-3 assembly; n=101, C=11 matching TCGA, both RNA sources); leak-free incremental-OOF probes (scratchpad); `learned/eval/ood_protein.py` (the aggregate scorer this replicates) | Falsifies the centrepiece of `CPTAC_PROTEIN_CHANNEL_PLAN.md` (see its **§0a**) and retires board **§A③**'s "sole remaining fundamental lever" framing. **Surviving axis (none depend on βᵗ):** `locus_cn_cptac` (BUILT, median r=0.997 vs ASCAT) · V1 δ-transportability · Bar-5 systematic (validates **β** via the `a·β` path — a different, easier claim; MH-83 = 7/7) · the **`a`** propagation slope — see MH-105 correction below. | MH-107 | **The CPTAC protein validation was CELL-COMPOSITION-CONFOUNDED — the flagship EMT/miR-200→ZEB1 protein result is compartment arithmetic — but this is a REFRAME, not a retraction: tumour-cell-INTRINSIC programs survive, stroma-loaded ones collapse, and the underlying edge is REAL.** **THE ERROR (stated correctly — an earlier framing of "the validator used the wrong C" was WRONG and is corrected):** the model's `deconv` block is **deliberately NOT a default** (`LEARNED_MODEL_METHODS §1`: *"tumour attribution = fractions; **gate/coupling = core only unless deconv requested**"*; *"Cancer-Epithelial is deliberately EXCLUDED — conditioning on the compartment the target is expressed in **over-controls the signal**"*) — because a miRNA acting through a cell-STATE shift PRODUCES a composition change, so composition is partly the MECHANISM. Conditioning therefore does **not** destroy the edge: composition **INFLATES** (confound) rather than **mediates**. **The true cell-autonomous effect is BOUNDED: 0.209 ≤ |ρ| ≤ 0.446. The miR-200→ZEB1 EDGE IS REAL; only its bulk-PROTEIN magnitude was confounded.** Within-stratum support: CPTAC protein in the low-CAF (most epithelial) third ρ=**−0.267** vs mid +0.025 / high −0.080 (n=30, p=0.15 — right sign, suggestive). | MH-108 | **The mRNA→protein propagation slope is CAUSALLY ~1:1 (a_IV = 0.893), not ~0.5 — the observational estimate is attenuated by 40% — and protein-complex BUFFERING survives causal estimation, becoming 2.4× STRONGER.** **Design (the mediation model's extension #6, previously unbuilt):** `a_OLS` (P regressed on M) is attenuated by measurement error in M and biased by any unmeasured mRNA↔protein confounder. **The target gene's own COPY NUMBER is a valid instrument for its mRNA** — CN drives the gene's transcription directly and has no plausible route to protein except through mRNA. | MH-108-C | **CORRECTION to MH-108: buffering is CAUSAL (confirmed) but it is NOT "2.4× stronger under IV" — that claim was a SELECTION ARTIFACT and is RETRACTED.** The error: I compared **Spearman(complex size, a_OLS) = −0.079 computed on ALL 9,196 genes** against **Spearman(complex size, a_IV) = −0.193 computed on the 4,346 F>10 genes** — **two different samples**. **On the SAME F>10 gene set: OLS ρ = −0.157 (p=1.8e-25) vs IV ρ = −0.136 (p=2.3e-19)** ⇒ **IV is slightly WEAKER, exactly as theory requires** (2SLS is *less efficient* than OLS — it discards the variation in M not explained by Z). The apparent "strengthening" is a **selection effect**: well-instrumented (high-CN-variance) genes simply show a stronger buffering relationship (ρ = −0.157 vs −0.061 over all genes). **WHAT STANDS:** (i) **causal `a_IV` = 0.893 vs `a_OLS` = 0.561** ⇒ mRNA reliability **λ = â_OLS/â_IV = 0.63** (≈37% of observed mRNA variance is measurement noise; independent bound from the two CPTAC RNA pipelines correlating at r=0.81 ⇒ λ ≤ 0.81, an upper bound since they share RNA/library) — **protein tracks mRNA ~1:1 CAUSALLY**; (ii) **buffering IS CAUSAL** — `a_IV` declines MONOTONICALLY with complex size: monomer **0.943** → 2–3 **0.970** → 4–12 **0.849** → 13–30 **0.763** → **≥31: 0.526** (MW p=8.8e-20; Spearman −0.136, p=2.3e-19). **⭐ STRONG INTERNAL VALIDATION of the errors-in-variables model:** the **IV/OLS ratio is CONSTANT across every stratum (1.55–1.68)** — exactly what `â_OLS = a·λ` predicts for a gene-independent λ: IV rescales by 1/λ≈1.6 and leaves the SHAPE intact. And **λ_g varies (IQR 0.46–0.82) but is INDEPENDENT of complex size (Spearman −0.042)** ⇒ attenuation does **not** bias the buffering contrast. | **S** (same-sample OLS-vs-IV comparison on 4,346 F>10 genes; monotone dose-response; the constant IV/OLS ratio across strata is a parameter-free prediction of the attenuation model, confirmed) | `output/learned/cptac_a_causal_iv.tsv` | **Corrects the "2.4× stronger" claim in MH-108.** The causal `a_IV` and the causal-buffering conclusions are UNCHANGED. |
| MH-109 | **THREE results, and the headline is a RETRACTION of my own: (a) protein-complex buffering is SYMMETRIC (deletion 0.53 vs amplification 0.55) — so a miRNA, which only represses, IS pushing in a buffered direction; (b) the MH-106 "mechanistic clincher" (miRNA effect reaches protein only where protein tracks mRNA) is RETRACTED — it does not hold; (c) consequently the protein-CONSEQUENCE filter is delivered as a mechanistically-grounded ANNOTATION, explicitly NOT validated in this cohort.** **(a) BUFFERING IS SYMMETRIC (premise-verification, not a discovery).** Complex buffering is usually attributed to *proteasomal degradation of EXCESS subunit* — which would protect only against OVER-expression and would mean a repressor like a miRNA is pushing in an UNBUFFERED direction. Tested by pooling **826,824 (gene,patient) cells over 8,450 genes** and estimating dP/dM separately in DELETED (CN<1.7, 14.1% of cells) vs AMPLIFIED (CN>2.3, 15.6%) cells, within CORUM complex-size strata: **complex≥31 vs monomer ratio = 0.53 on DELETION and 0.55 on AMPLIFICATION — essentially identical**, with a clean monotone gradient in BOTH directions (deleted 0.394→0.209; amplified 0.487→0.270). ⇒ **complex subunits are damped against BOTH excess and deficit** ⇒ the "a miRNA pushes in an unbuffered direction" objection is REFUTED. (The *concept* of bidirectional buffering is modelled in the literature — Biology Direct 2015; this is an empirical quantification in human cancer tissue, and the check that licenses (c).) **(b) ⛔ RETRACTION of MH-106's "mechanistic clincher".** I claimed "the miRNA effect reaches protein ONLY where protein tracks mRNA" (a_g<0.15 → no transfer; a_g 0.55–0.80 → retention 0.392, p=3e-4) and called it a free internal-consistency check. **It is an ARTIFACT of coarse binning + an unstable ratio-of-medians "retention" statistic.** The **continuous** test: on the 204 genes with a usable CN instrument, Spearman with protein-repression strength is **a_OLS +0.010 (p=0.88) · a_IV +0.032 (p=0.65) · λ (measurement reliability) −0.003 (p=0.97)**; partial a_IV|λ = +0.031 (p=0.66). Over all 490 genes a_OLS gives ρ=+0.103 (p=0.022) — **but instrument strength F (pure CN variance, a nuisance) predicts equally well (ρ=+0.114, p=0.011)** ⇒ **NOT interpretable as biology.** **UNSUPPORTED ≠ DISPROVEN:** the miRNA→protein signal is at the NOISE FLOOR (median ρ = −0.02 against a per-gene SE ≈ 0.10 at n=101), so a *modulation* of it is undetectable by construction. **(c) THE PROTEIN-CONSEQUENCE ANNOTATION (delivered, honestly labelled).** Per-edge score = the TARGET's CAUSAL propagation slope `a_IV` (MH-108). Coverage: **1,768/5,938 HE edges (30%)** have a target with a usable instrument (F>10); of those, **BUFFERED (a_IV<0.40) 157 (8.9%) · partial 669 (37.8%) · CONSEQUENTIAL (a_IV>0.80) 942 (53.3%)**. ⚠ **This is an a-priori, mechanistically-grounded PRIOR — NOT a validated filter:** its premise (that a_g modulates whether repression reaches protein) is near-definitional but **cannot be demonstrated in CPTAC at n=101** (see (b)). It is testable in a larger proteogenomic cohort. | **A/P** ((a) is a solid pooled measurement = S; (b) is a rigorous same-sample retraction = S; (c) is an ANNOTATION whose predictive claim is explicitly UNVALIDATED = P) | `output/learned/cptac_a_by_cn_direction.tsv`, `output/learned/{edge,program}_protein_consequence.tsv`, `output/learned/cptac_a_causal_iv.tsv` | **RETRACTS the "mechanistic clincher" of MH-106.** Verifies the premise for a protein-consequence filter, then declines to claim the filter is validated. | MH-111 | **The composition confound, resolved at ARM → FAMILY → GENE: (a) the Wu-major fractions are a SIMPLEX, so the design's "hold Cancer-Epithelial out" is mathematically an ILLUSION (R²=1.000) — but EMPIRICALLY BENIGN (purity contributes 0.9% of the loss; CPE already absorbs it); (b) the confound is the MESENCHYMAL/VASCULAR axis — CAFs 10.7% · Endothelial 6.3% · Myeloid 5.6% · PVL 4.1% — while ADAPTIVE IMMUNE contributes exactly 0.0%; (c) it CONCENTRATES in ONE family (95% of a gene's loss); (d) but UNLIKE the proliferation and host confounds it does NOT localise to an arm — seed-family members are compartment-HOMOGENEOUS.** **(a) THE SIMPLEX.** `LEARNED_MODEL_METHODS §1` holds `Cancer Epithelial` OUT of the `deconv` block because *"conditioning on the compartment the target is expressed in over-controls the signal"*. **But the 9 Wu-major fractions sum to EXACTLY 1.0** (sd=0.0000, 1,059 participants) ⇒ **R²(Cancer-Epithelial ~ the 8 'held-out' lineages) = 1.0000** ⇒ conditioning on the 8 DETERMINES the malignant fraction. **The stated protection is an illusion.** **⭐ But the CONSEQUENCE is benign** — compositional reparametrisation (m = malignant fraction = the PURITY axis; w = the 8 renormalised to the non-malignant simplex = the purity-FREE stromal MIX) gives: **conditioning on PURITY alone → median retention 0.991 (0.9% loss); on the STROMAL MIX alone → 0.669 (33.1% loss); on BOTH → 0.651 (34.9%)**. ⇒ **the malignant fraction contributes ~1% — CPE (already in core C) has absorbed it. The over-control the design feared is NOT happening.** **(b) WHICH CELLS (add one lineage at a time, 2,302 couplings):** **CAFs 10.7% loss · Endothelial 6.3% · Myeloid 5.6% · PVL 4.1%** · Normal-Epithelial 1.5% · B-cells 0.6% · **T-cells 0.0% · Plasmablasts 0.0%**. ⇒ **the confound is the MESENCHYMAL/VASCULAR/MYELOID axis, NOT lymphocytes** — the four stromal lineages co-vary as ONE axis (Σ individual 28.8% ≈ joint 33.1%). **(c) GENE-LEVEL ALLOCATION (Q1).** Because the Shapley of a LINEAR aggregate is exactly β_f, allocation is ADDITIVE ⇒ `gene_retention = Σ_f β_f^deconv / Σ_f β_f` (a SHARE-WEIGHTED aggregate, not a naive gene-level re-fit). **1,502 genes: median 0.800 — cell_intrinsic 60.5% · partial 29.5% · composition_explained 10.0%.** **⭐ The loss is CONCENTRATED: the single worst family carries 95% (median) of a gene's composition loss** — the same architecture as MH-100 (proliferation, arm-concentration 0.85) and MH-101 (host). **(d) ARM → FAMILY (Q3) — an INFORMATIVE NEGATIVE.** Compartment identity is computed per ARM (corr(arm dose, lineage fraction)); **within-family SPREAD of compartment lock is median 0.0121** (90th pct 0.17) ⇒ **seed-family members are compartment-HOMOGENEOUS**, and all three arm summaries predict family retention identically (dose-dominant ρ=−0.132 · dose-weighted −0.134 · unweighted −0.129). ⇒ **going arm-first buys NOTHING for composition — the FAMILY is the right unit.** **This is a genuine architectural CONTRAST: the proliferation confound (MH-100) localises to ONE arm (miR-93-5p) and the host confound (MH-101) follows the DOSE-DOMINANT arm — because those are LOCUS properties (host gene, replication locus) on which same-seed members DIVERGE. Same-seed members sit at DIFFERENT LOCI ⇒ independent locus CN ⇒ an **exogenous** handle immune to BOTH abundance and composition. At n=96 we exclude agreement >60% but not ~55%. **WHAT WOULD ANSWER IT: more CN power, a BREAST chimeric eCLIP (not HEK293T), or per-arm knockdown — a DATA ask, not a modelling one.** **(5) ATTRIBUTION REFINES, DOESN'T OVERTURN:** detection-gated, **97.7% concordant** with the family model on WHICH family wins; | MH-123 | **⭐⭐ THE PER-EDGE NULL IS MISCALIBRATED — the theoretical t-null is wrong by 3–4× in SCALE, so per-edge miRNA→target discovery in TCGA bulk is essentially impossible at n=1041, and the HE signal is a SET-LEVEL DISTRIBUTIONAL SHIFT, not per-edge significance.** **THE DIAGNOSIS.** A SITE-FREE class — (arm,gene) pairs with NO TargetScan context++ site AND no scanMiR duplex (repression > −0.5), i.e. pairs that **cannot repress by any modelled mechanism** but carry the SAME population structure. Measured (TCGA, n=1041, 60,000 site-free pairs): under the theoretical t-null **35.3% of them are "FDR-significant" (q<0.05, ρ<0) under core C and 25.0% under composition-adjusted C** — a 25–35% false-positive rate at a nominal 5% FDR. **THE FITTED NULL** (per arm-abundance quintile, robust median / 1.4826·MAD): theoretical would be μ=0, **sd=0.0311**; measured site-free null is **μ₀ = −0.048…−0.021, sd₀ = 0.105…0.132 (core C)** and **μ₀ = −0.027…−0.011, sd₀ = 0.083…0.097 (composition-adjusted)** ⇒ **the real null is 3–4× WIDER than the t-distribution assumes AND its mean is NEGATIVE** (a global miRNA↔mRNA anti-correlation from shared population structure). Composition adjustment shrinks both shift and width but does **NOT** close the gap ⇒ the confound is only partly compartmental. **RECALIBRATED COUNTS** (composition-adjusted C, Efron empirical null, BH q<0.05): **HE 1,313 (31.3%) → 1 (0.0%)** · ORPHAN 3,891 (83.5%) → 200 (4.3%) · site-free 14,971 (25.0%) → 0 (calibrated by construction). **⭐⭐ THE RECONCILIATION — THIS DOES *NOT* SAY THE EDGES ARE FAKE (both are true):** HE edges ARE genuinely enriched — abundance-matched (log2RPM>4) vs the site-free class **OR 1.41, Fisher p=2.5e-13, and it SURVIVES composition adjustment (OR 1.43 core → 1.41 deconv ⇒ THE CURATED SIGNAL WAS NEVER COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC)** — but a typical HE edge has **ρ≈−0.15 against σ₀=0.09 ⇒ z≈−1.4, p≈0.08**: real, but nowhere near surviving FDR across 4,000 tests. The ORPHAN's 200 survivors are CIRCULAR SELECTION showing through (nominated FOR extreme ρ). **⭐ SEQUENCE CANDIDACY IS MOSTLY COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC, CURATION IS NOT — composition adjustment does not wash everything out, it SEPARATES:** predicted-site (CAND) vs site-free (FAKE) **OR 1.36 (p=7e-24) → 1.11 (p=8e-04)** under composition, while **HE vs FAKE is UNCHANGED (1.43→1.41)** and HE vs CAND goes from n.s. (OR 1.05, p=0.12) to **significant (OR 1.27, p=5e-08)**. **⚠ THE FIRST IMPLEMENTATION WAS BROKEN AND THE GIVEAWAY WAS HE→0:** counting EXCEEDANCES against a 12k-per-bin null is RESOLUTION-LIMITED (min p = 1/N ≈ 8e-5), so BH across 4,194 tests (needing p<1.2e-5) can NEVER fire; fixed with the Efron form (FIT the null's location+scale, score against N(μ₀,σ₀), continuous p). **⇒ CONSEQUENCE (load-bearing): every per-edge FDR count in this subproject — incl. MH-114's survival grid, `discovery`'s in_partial ρ<−0.15 gate, `dossier`'s `couples` — is inflated by a miscalibrated null.** The defensible readouts are (a) SET-LEVEL enrichment vs an abundance-matched site-free null and (b) AGGREGATE/gene-level PREDICTION (which does not depend on the per-edge null at all). **RESERVE "discovery" LANGUAGE FOR THE AGGREGATE LANE.** **⚠ HONEST COUNTER-CAVEAT:** the site-free class certainly contains SOME real non-canonical targets, which widens the null and makes it **CONSERVATIVE** — the truth is between the theoretical null (clearly far too liberal) and this one, but a 3–4× scale error is not a small correction. | **S** (an impossible-by-construction class carrying the same population structure; the null is FITTED, not assumed; the enrichment leg is independently abundance-matched and composition-checked) | scratchpad `empnull.py`, `hf2.py`; ledger MH-123. Wire the site-free null into `discovery`/`dossier` as the calibration standard. |

And the real defect is `readouts.share`.** `METHODS §5` ("canonical attribution = bagged NNLS") and `SYNTHESIS §6b` ("dense posterior → attribution") describe **different objects**: **MAGNITUDE** (who delivers how much) = the dense Gibbs posterior mean `beta` ⇒ **§6b is right**; **IDENTITY** (who owns the gene) = `attribution.shapley_identity` (LMG on R²), which needs **fixed weights**, and those come from `states.canonical_M` = **bagged NNLS** ⇒ **§5 is right, for that job**. **THE CODE WAS ALREADY COHERENT** (verified): `attribution.identity_vs_magnitude:131` and `bayes_shapley_identity:91` both call `ST.canonical_M`, and the latter uses it exactly as `RATIONALE §2e` prescribed — **NNLS fixes the support + signs, the Gibbs draws supply the width** = §2e's "deployable form: NNLS-style point estimate + EB posterior sd". **⭐ §2e's MECHANISM IS NOW CONFIRMED ON THE SHIPPED TABLE** (`readouts_edges.tsv`, n=5,117): the half-normal slab `N⁺(0,ν²)` has a **strictly positive mean**, so an un-informed family **cannot be zeroed** — it relaxes to the prior. **beta == 0 in 0/5,117 (0.00%); 100% are > 0**, where NNLS returns exact zeros. **⇒ `readouts.share` (= β_f/Σβ, labelled "ATTRIB / IDENTITY — the Bayesian Shapley" at `readouts.py:16-21,171`) IS NOT IDENTITY AND IS NOT SAFE:** (a) for an ADDITIVE value function Shapley is trivially β_f ⇒ it **splits nothing under collinearity** (MH-122); (b) its denominator is **majority-unidentified mass** — **POOLED 73.0% of all β-mass sits on edges with \|z\|≤2**, per-gene **median 55.5%**, and the distribution is **BIMODAL**: 21.2% of genes carry <1% such mass while **42.7% (662/1,549) carry 100%** — **452 of those 662 are SINGLE-FAMILY genes, where β ≡ uniform by construction anyway**. Restricted to `n_fam≥3` the median falls to **32.0%**. ⇒ **the genome-wide table has NO true identity column**; true identity requires `shapley_identity` (per-gene, 400 perms). ⚠ **PRECISION: `unidentified` (\|z\|≤2) is NOT proof of zero signal** — it means *not distinguishable from the prior* (a large β with a large sd is unidentified too; the unidentified edges' **mean** β ≈ the identified edges' mean despite medians differing 8.6×). So 73.0% is the mass the estimator **cannot separate from prior drift**, not 73% proven-fake — which is sufficient for the verdict: feeding it to a Shapley credits families the model cannot distinguish from noise. ⬜ **Open: relabel/gate `readouts.share`** (MH-119's `share_abs`/`share_reliable` flags mitigate but do not fix the label). | **S** (own end-to-end; reproduced from the shipped artifact; code paths verified by reading) | `eval/_share_drift_check.py`; `output/learned/readouts_edges.tsv`; `learned/attribution.py:91,131`, `learned/readouts.py:16-21,171` |

| MH-140 | **⭐ THE TABLE HAS A TRUE IDENTITY COLUMN FOR THE FIRST TIME — and it disagrees with magnitude in 1 of every 4 multi-family genes.** MH-138 established the genome-wide table had **no identity readout**: `share` (= β_f/Σβ) was labelled *"ATTRIB / IDENTITY — the Bayesian Shapley"* but for the **ADDITIVE** aggregate Shapley(f) ≡ β_f ⇒ it splits nothing under collinearity, and `top_family_identity` was `argmax(share)` ≡ `argmax(β)` (Σβ is a per-gene constant) — **MEASURED IDENTICAL to `top_family_magnitude` in 99.35% of 1,549 genes**, the 0.65% differing only by Jensen (share is E[β_f/Σβ] per draw, not E[β_f]/E[Σβ]). **BUILT:** `readouts.py` now emits **`identity`** = `attribution.shapley_identity` (Shapley/LMG on **R²(Xz·m, yr)** — a **NON-additive** value function ⇒ collinear overlap genuinely SPLIT), with **m = bagged NNLS** on the SAME prepped design (MH-138: the Gibbs mean cannot zero an un-informed family). `share`→**`beta_frac`** (demoted to MAGNITUDE), `share_deconv`→`beta_frac_deconv`, plus **`identity_deconv`** (the cell-intrinsic identity the docstring always promised) and `identity_eq_magnitude`. **⭐ RESULT: WHO ≠ HOW-MUCH.** Agreement **85.3%** genome-wide but **75.2% among the 819 MULTI-family genes** (the 730 single-family genes agree by construction) ⇒ **in ~25% of genes where the question is non-trivial, the family that OWNS the coupling is not the family with the largest β.** **The MH-138 mechanism at genome scale: `m_nnls` is exactly 0 in 1,623/5,117 families (31.7%); `beta` is exactly 0 in 0/5,117.** **⭐ AND IDENTITY CAN SAY "UNDEFINED": 213/1,549 genes (13.8%) have NaN identity — in 100% of them NNLS zeroed EVERY family** ⇒ no coupling to distribute; NaN is the honest value, and it is a statement `beta`/`beta_frac` **structurally cannot make** (β>0 everywhere). Among the 1,336 defined genes identity sums to **exactly 1.0000**. **CONTROL — the change is PURELY ADDITIVE: `beta`, `beta_sd`, `z`, `pip_dense`, `pip_discovery` are BIT-IDENTICAL to the pre-change table (max\|Δ\| = 0.000e+00 on all five, 5,117 rows).** ⚡ **`shapley_identity` optimized 8.2×–9.7×** (it rebuilt the aggregate from scratch per permutation step ⇒ O(n_perm·n·p²/2); now incremental ⇒ O(n_perm·n·p)): ESR1 p=15 476→58 ms, PTEN p=64 2405→247 ms, **verified output-identical (max\|Δφ\| ≤ 7.6e-17)** ⇒ identity costs **6.5%** of a readouts run (was 35.3%); full run **3.0 min**. ⚠ **SCOPE unchanged (MH-126): the model RANKS the canonical family above chance but does NOT NAME it** — read the ranking, not the argmax.
